Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any structure module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 99. The projective resolution has length at most 623 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_99(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 99. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^623(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.975 (Perturbation Bound)

Statement: Under the perturbation bound assumption on structure structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 387. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 100 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 387 is immediate from the perturbation bound property. For the inductive step, we decompose the structure structure using the perturbation bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 100. Each subproblem has a solution by the perturbation bound fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
